# The catalogue

Where everything is, who made it and when, at site v0.1.1309. Generated from the build and git by librarian@PrompTugal.dinis; the data is `board/catalogue.json`. "Made" is the first commit that touched the source (its agent and time, UTC); "changed" the last. 1530 pages (256 site, 66 article, 120 company, 6 list, 248 event, 165 newsroom, 13 decision, 269 organisation, 285 person, 85 place, 17 topic), 1291 board files, 19 desks, 30 passes, 27 PrompTugal agents.

## Ideas, questions and concepts (the ideas ledger)

From `board/guidance/*ideas-ledger*.md` (conductor@'s reading of Dinis's night memos; the memos win where they differ): 494 ideas (213 standing rule, 52 decided, 133 now, 54 later, 27 now (done), 13 question, 2 tomorrow), 13 questions, 88 concepts. Every one is a record in `board/catalogue.json`, findable by its id (`idea:PB-03`), its words or its owner. Below, the ones most easily lost: the ideas for later and those not captured anywhere yet.

### Questions Dinis asked

| # | His question | Where it stands |
|---|---|---|
| Q1 | how many files and gigabytes of data they already have, and the runs, and how often they're doing the runs | Partly: REALITY.md says Vicky's researcher uses pack mode, four runs a day. The size in files and gigabytes is not answered anywhere on the board. Owner: backup@ or observability@. |
| Q2 | I'm going to give you a whole bunch of brief now on the quality gates and the sort of the research and going deep | His promised notes on quality gates have not arrived as such (board/WS-177-plan.md waits for them). Ask Dinis. |
| Q3 | I have a bunch of ideas on how to do this, but let's iterate | His ideas for the graphs' next level were not given. Ask at the next stand-up. |
| Q4 | The only question is whether it gets captured and it gets saved | Open: the profile lives in the reader's browser; whether and where it is saved (a vault, sealed) is not decided. |
| Q5 | The user area's name ("you", "logged in", "user") | Not decided. designer@ and sherpa@ to propose on a decision page. |
| Q6 | A graphs agent, "a roof.sgit agent ... or maybe we have both | Half answered: dev.graphs@ works in ruv.sgit's style; no ruv.sgit agent ("the queen") was started. Dinis to say if he wants one. |
| Q7 | a UX expert, you know, do we need that? You know, like, do we have enough coverage of these domains? | Not answered directly; sherpa@ and designer@ cover usability. The conductor to answer in a brief. |
| Q8 | He named "news.sgit.ai" as the model for the articles page | The board reads it as newsroom.sgit.ai (which he names at the end of the memo). Confirm with Dinis if news.sgit.ai is a separate site. |
| Q9 | Do we have those hourly articles | No: the updates section (p/the-updates-section) is queued; the first-night debrief was written instead. |
| Q10 | who actually has read this?" (the synthetic findings) | Partly: findings get owners and decisions on the synthetic readers page; who read them is not yet shown. |
| Q11 | The 200 Brazilians "coming to Porto | Check against the sources: the summit is in Lisbon; the delegation's facts are in the Brazil project. dev.verify@ and journalist@. |
| Q12 | customers@ "which I want to give a couple specific briefs | customers@ runs and has a brief (the personal newsreel), but it is not in CLAUDE.md's team table; the conductor to propose the row to Dinis. |
| Q13 | how to become a publisher in Portugal | The goal is in p/becoming-a-publication; the answer is not yet on the board. journalist@. |
